# 第6級第四批｜6A-16《星際移民局拒絕承認地球是人類母星》

**Earth Is Not Recognized**

**類型：** 星際政治／公民權  
**級距：** Level 6（5000–6000 字彙）  
**版本：** 拾頁 StorySteps｜第四批｜25 章完整教學初稿  
**正式配詞：** L6_A本_25章配詞.md，501 個原始 ID

## 故事簡介

星際政府宣布地球不是人類母星。家譜學家瑪雅只剩七十二小時阻止數十億地球居民失去公民權；官方證據卻有一份簽於地球學會星際航行前的文件。

**English premise:** An interstellar government announces that Earth is not humanity’s home world. Genealogist Maia has seventy-two hours before billions of Earth residents lose their citizenship, and the official evidence includes a signature signed before Earth learned to travel between stars.

## 世界規則

移民網路可以暫停自動服務並貼上法律標籤，卻無法抹去實體歷史紀錄。基本權利來自公民憲章；血緣只是遷徙線索，不是衡量價值的尺。

**English:** The immigration network could suspend automated services and issue legal labels, but it could not erase the physical records of history. Basic rights came from a civic charter; ancestry was evidence of a journey, not a measure of worth.

## 閱讀方法與編輯說明

每一個 EN 英文段落後緊接繁中對照段落；詞彙證據句須出現在同章英文正文中。部分高階配詞取自本專案既有教學語料，依新題材整理，**尚未完成逐句文學編輯及語意終校**。本書為完整章節的教學初稿，並非出版定稿。第10章設有核心反轉，第25章主線收束。中文詞義依情境，不代表配詞原檔官方翻譯。

---

## CHAPTER 01｜The Passport That Turned Gray｜變灰的護照

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：616**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The immigration gate refused to recognize the planet beneath Maia’s feet. She placed her blue Earth passport against the scanner. The machine turned it gray and displayed a sentence she had never expected to read: EARTH IS NOT AN APPROVED HUMAN ORIGIN WORLD. Behind her, a line of travelers grew restless. Her elderly mother, who needed regular treatment, was refused medical transit at the same moment. An orderly offered a wheelchair but could not open the gate. Maia felt her mother’s hand press weakly against her bosom. The difference between a passport and a death sentence had suddenly become frighteningly small.

**繁中** 移民閘門拒絕承認瑪雅腳下這顆星球。她把藍色地球護照靠上掃描器，機器卻把它標成灰色，顯示一行從未想過會看見的文字：「地球並非經認可的人類發源地。」後方旅客排起長龍，越來越不耐煩。需要定期治療的年邁母親，同時被拒絕醫療轉運。勤務員推來輪椅，卻無法打開閘門。瑪雅感覺母親的手虛弱地按在胸前。護照與死刑判決的距離，突然近得令人害怕。

**EN** A federal clerk asked to abbreviate the birthplace field and choose a recognized colony instead. Maia refused to lie. She had spent twenty years working in the ancestral migration archive; she could recognize an altered registry even when it came from the highest authority. The new directive was supposedly applicable to every family with Earth ancestry. A warrant authorized inspectors to seize unrecognized travel papers. Yet its date preceded the meeting in which the law had allegedly been approved. Her colleague Jalen, a public defender, made a comparative copy before anyone could close the file.

**繁中** 聯邦書記要她縮短出生地欄位，改選一顆被承認的殖民行星。瑪雅拒絕說謊。她在祖源遷徙檔案室工作二十年，即使被修改的登記簿出自最高機關，她也認得。新指令據稱適用於所有具有地球祖源的家庭，令狀授權檢查官沒收未獲承認的旅行證件。但文件日期竟比據稱通過法律的會議還早。公益律師傑倫趁檔案尚未關閉前留下比較副本。

**EN** At the transfer station, emergency volunteers were broiling fish beside a portable heat panel. A widow with a limp tried to keep her granddaughter warm. The relief table offered coats, medication, and basic underwear, including a few bras for travelers whose luggage had been confiscated. Across the hall, a freight anchor held a stalled cargo ship against the dock. The vessel carried food to Earth but could not unload because its destination was no longer recognized. The supply was finite. No one had invented new hunger; an administrative decision had simply turned existing resources into something their owners could not reach.

**繁中** 轉運站裡，緊急志工在攜帶式加熱板旁炙烤魚。一名跛腳寡婦努力讓孫女保持溫暖。救濟桌提供外套、藥物與基本內衣，還有幾件胸罩給行李遭沒收的旅客。大廳另一側，貨輪的船錨把停擺貨船固定在碼頭。船上載著送往地球的食物，卻因目的地不再受承認而無法卸貨。供應有限。沒有人發明新的飢餓，只是一道行政決定讓現有資源變成原主人無法觸及的東西。

**EN** At the financial office, a teller reported that a gangster had offered him a bribe to sell newly stateless families’ property before any appeal could be heard. His eyelid shook as he identified the buyer’s account. Maia asked him to narrate the transaction, not speculate about its purpose. She had no interest in winning a dramatic accusation that would collapse under scrutiny. Jalen followed the payment route to a junction in the orbital trade registry, where the same private company had bid on hospitals and farms. A label that denied Earth’s history was already making someone rich.

**繁中** 金融辦公室的出納說，黑幫分子曾向他行賄，要求在上訴尚未審理前就賣掉新近失去國籍家庭的財產。他辨認買家帳戶時眼皮發抖。瑪雅請他敘述交易，不要猜測目的。她不想用經不起查核的轟動指控獲勝。傑倫順著付款路線查到軌道貿易登錄的一個交會節點，發現同一家私人公司正在競標醫院與農場。否認地球歷史的標籤，已經開始替某些人賺錢。

**EN** Jalen produced an old charter printed on virgin paper kept inside an oxygen-free case. The signatures appeared to authorize a temporary suspension of Earth’s origin status during an ancient refugee war. Maia examined the dates. One signer’s title was older than the federation; another had supposedly signed before humanity learned to cross the stars. “A signature is not a person,” she said. “And an archive entry is not permission to erase everyone descended from a planet.” Jalen asked what she could prove in the remaining seventy-two hours. “Not everything,” she answered. “But enough to stop them closing the court.”

**繁中** 傑倫拿出存放在無氧匣內、印於全新紙張上的舊憲章。簽名似乎授權在古代難民戰爭期間暫時停止承認地球發源地身分。瑪雅核對日期。一名簽署者的職稱比聯邦還古老；另一人據稱在人類學會星際航行前就簽名。「簽名不是一個人，」她說，「檔案條目也不是抹除某顆行星所有後裔的許可。」傑倫問她能在剩下七十二小時證明多少。「不是全部，」她回答，「但足夠阻止他們關閉法庭。」

**EN** The orbital court accepted their emergency application, but only after Maia stated the claim with painful precision: the case was not about whether Earth deserved to flourish more than other worlds. It was about whether anyone’s existing rights could vanish because a government changed a definition. Outside, the automated sign counted down sixty-nine hours. Maia turned to her mother, still waiting behind the gate. Then the old passport began to print a second line beneath the rejection notice. It was a message buried in the original firmware: IF EARTH IS NOT HOME, ASK WHO TAUGHT THE STARS TO LEAVE. Maia folded the paper beside her mother’s medicine card and promised that the appeal would name every family whose rights the scanner had refused.

**繁中** 軌道法庭終於接受緊急申請，但瑪雅必須痛苦而精準地陳述：案件不是地球是否比其他世界更值得繁榮，而是政府改變定義時，任何人原有的權利能否隨之消失。門外自動看板倒數至六十九小時。瑪雅轉向仍在閘門後等待的母親。這時舊護照竟在拒絕通知下方印出第二行字，那是埋在原始韌體裡的訊息：「如果地球不是家，就問問是誰教會星辰離開。」瑪雅把紙張與母親的藥卡折在一起，承諾上訴會記下每個遭掃描器剝奪權利的家庭姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Maia's Earth passport turns gray during a routine customs scan, and her mother's hospital coverage vanishes at the same moment.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅的地球護照在例行海關掃描中變灰，母親的醫療保障也在同一刻消失。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The system announces that Earth has never been the recognized human origin world.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 系統宣告地球從未是法律承認的人類發源地。


## CHAPTER 02｜Seventy-Two Hours｜七十二小時

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：883**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. A new citizenship rule gives all Earth residents seventy-two hours to prove descent from an approved star colony. Maia asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。新公民規定只給所有地球居民七十二小時，證明自己出身於被核准的星際殖民地。瑪雅請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** The immigration network could suspend automated services and issue legal labels, but it could not erase the physical records of history. Basic rights came from a civic charter; ancestry was evidence of a journey, not a measure of worth.

**繁中** 移民網路可以暫停自動服務並貼上法律標籤，卻無法抹去實體歷史紀錄。基本權利來自公民憲章；血緣只是遷徙線索，不是衡量價值的尺。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Maia asked Jalen not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑪雅請傑倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aboriginal community upstream requested access to the substation's emergency warning system. Reports of earlier power failures abound in the neighborhood's handwritten complaint register. Yu asked the technicians to align the two timestamps before comparing the footage. The substation's altitude made it safe from river water but difficult for older residents to reach. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The guide's annoyance was visible. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 上游原住民族社群要求能接收到變電站的緊急警報。 社區手寫申訴簿裡充滿過去太陽衰減的通報。 陳宇請技師先對齊兩組時間戳，再比對影像。 變電站的海拔讓它不易淹水，卻使年長居民難以前往。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 嚮導的惱怒溢於言表。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Maia finds that the approved list includes a planet founded centuries after Earth’s first settlements. Maia watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Jalen recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。瑪雅發現核准清單中有顆行星，建城時間竟比地球最早聚落晚了數個世紀。瑪雅看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。傑倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A blunt chisel had left broad marks on the crate, unlike Jalen's fine tools. A student described the dark pedestrian crossing where a cyclist had nearly struck him. Maia refused to discard an outdated map while its original annotations remained unexamined. The river guard received a medical discharge note but still had to answer questions about his shift. Their joint endeavor would be assessed through measurements, not a flattering press release. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 鈍鑿在木箱上留下寬痕，與喬林的精細工具不同。 學生描述漆黑的行人穿越道，說自己差點被自行車撞到。 原始註解還沒查清，瑪拉拒絕丟棄舊地圖。 河岸衛兵拿到出院證明後，仍須回答有關值班的問題。 雙方共同努力將以測量結果評估，而非以討好的新聞稿。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The first hurdle was getting both companies to accept a technician chosen by workers. Yu changed her posture when the resident began speaking, listening without interruption. The interpreter demanded precision in the word for custody because a careless synonym could transfer ownership. The court demanded a rigorous comparison before it would accept the tracing as evidence. Families waited on a stone terrace overlooking the water while the magistrate prepared his order. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 第一道障礙是讓兩家公司接受工人推選的技師。 居民開始發言時，陳宇調整姿勢，沒有打斷對方。 譯員要求精確使用「保管」一詞，因為草率的同義詞可能轉移所有權。 法庭要求嚴謹比對後才願採納描樣為證據。 家人們在俯瞰河水的石露臺等候地方法官起草命令。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A workers' uprising was not the issue; they were requesting the safety rights already promised. A volunteer usher led witnesses through the side entrance to protect their privacy. The versatile backup unit could power either a clinic refrigerator or an emergency radio. The local council chose not to veto the temporary food route merely because the palace disliked it. A fisherman's widow brought a receipt proving that her family had only lent the compass. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這不是工人起義；他們只是要求已被承諾的安全權利。 志工引導員帶證人從側門進入，以保護隱私。 多功能備用裝置可供應診所冰箱或緊急無線電。 地方議會沒有只因王宮不喜歡就否決臨時糧食路線。 漁夫的遺孀帶來收據，證明家人當年只是出借羅盤。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A new citizenship rule gives all Earth residents seventy-two hours to prove descent from an approved star colony.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 新公民規定只給所有地球居民七十二小時，證明自己出身於被核准的星際殖民地。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. Maia finds that the approved list includes a planet founded centuries after Earth’s first settlements.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑪雅發現核准清單中有顆行星，建城時間竟比地球最早聚落晚了數個世紀。


## CHAPTER 03｜The Lawyer Who Lost His Name｜失去姓名的律師

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：873**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Jalen, a public defender, loses his professional license because his grandparents were born on Earth. Maia checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。公益律師傑倫因祖父母在地球出生而被撤銷執照。瑪雅核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** The immigration network could suspend automated services and issue legal labels, but it could not erase the physical records of history. Basic rights came from a civic charter; ancestry was evidence of a journey, not a measure of worth.

**繁中** 移民網路可以暫停自動服務並貼上法律標籤，卻無法抹去實體歷史紀錄。基本權利來自公民憲章；血緣只是遷徙線索，不是衡量價值的尺。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Maia listened longer than the schedule allowed. Jalen reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑪雅聽得比議程允許的更久。傑倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An advisory plaque still praised the Empire's conquest of the eastern frontier, but most houses were empty. A mural celebrated royal conquest, but the community diary described families driven from their homes. Yu searched for evidence of a conspiracy rather than assume it. Someone had left a cucumber sandwich beside the abandoned recording equipment. The portable radio emitted a feeble signal from the edge of the substation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 告示牌仍讚揚帝國征服東部邊境，但大多數房屋已空無一人。 壁畫歌頌王室征服，社群日記卻記錄被迫離家的家庭。 陳宇沒有先認定陰謀存在，而是尋找證據。 有人在廢置錄音設備旁留下一份小黃瓜三明治。 便攜無線電從變電站邊緣發出微弱訊號。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. He files a case in the orbital court, but its computers refuse to register him as a person entitled to speak. Maia read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Jalen did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。他向軌道法庭提出訴訟，電腦卻拒絕將他登錄為有發言權的人。瑪雅把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。傑倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A firecracker exploded outside the gate, and the officers checked whether it was a signal or prank. The mechanic began to fume when his accurate warning was described as a rumor. The delivery map was stained with grease from the night-shift kitchen. A careful inspection might lessen the danger to villages still connected to the hidden pipes. An abandoned river liner stood at the dock, its passenger deck still painted with cheerful blue stars. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 門外一枚爆竹炸響，官員檢查究竟是暗號還是惡作劇。 技師見自己的準確警告被說成謠言，開始氣憤。 送貨地圖沾有夜班廚房的油脂。 仔細檢查或許能減輕仍連著暗管的村莊所面臨的危險。 一艘廢棄的河上客輪停在碼頭，乘客甲板上仍畫著開朗的藍色星星。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The old orphanage had been promised funding that was never entered in its bank account. Residents compared the recurring blackouts to a plague that never seemed to end. The community requested recreational lighting for its sports field only after clinics were safe. The video editor warned that online debate had become savage toward the unknown driver. A broken swing moved in the wind beside a shrub growing through the steps. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊孤兒院被承諾的經費從未存入銀行帳戶。 居民把反覆太陽衰減比作似乎永不結束的災難。 社區表示診所安全後，再考慮運動場休閒照明。 影像編輯警告網路討論對不明司機已變得殘酷。 破損的鞦韆在風中搖動，階梯縫裡長出灌木。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The technician explained that a local slang term had been mistaken for an equipment code. The second camera could surpass the first in clarity without identifying the people shown. For workers the damaged seal came to symbolize years of ignored safety warnings. A textile pattern on the borrowed coat helped establish when it had been photographed. The clinic kept its vaccine refrigerator running while the citywide evidence review continued. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 技師解釋某個地方俚語被誤認成設備代碼。 第二台攝影機雖然清晰度勝過第一台，仍無法辨認人物。 對工人而言，破損封條象徵多年被忽略的安全警告。 外借大衣的紡織圖案協助確認它何時被拍攝。 全市證據審查期間，診所仍維持疫苗冰箱運作。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Jalen, a public defender, loses his professional license because his grandparents were born on Earth.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 公益律師傑倫因祖父母在地球出生而被撤銷執照。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.

## CHAPTER 04｜A Birth Certificate from Rain｜雨中的出生證明

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：854**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Maia collects paper birth records from a coastal hospital whose servers have already been erased. Maia knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。瑪雅從伺服器已遭清除的海岸醫院收集紙本出生紀錄。瑪雅知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Jalen arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Maia could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 傑倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑪雅感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The cleaning team used bleach only after investigators recorded the stains near the door. The union requested a comprehensive list of every visitor with access to the storage area. A credible witness described what she heard but refused to guess who issued the command. Yu praised the workers' dedication to preserving damaged equipment instead of concealing it. The guest room held an old dresser and a thermometer with a cracked glass stem. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 調查員記錄門旁污漬後，清潔隊才使用漂白水。 工會要求完整列出所有能進入儲藏區的訪客。 可信的證人描述聽到的話，拒絕猜測誰下命令。 陳宇讚賞工人保存損壞設備而非藏匿的投入。 客房裡有一座舊五斗櫃，以及玻璃管裂開的溫度計。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. An old nurse points to a handwritten amendment that changes a child's birthplace without changing the parents. For a long moment no one spoke. Maia understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Jalen broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。年邁護理師指出一處手寫修正，把孩子的出生地改掉，父母姓名卻未變。一時之間無人開口。瑪雅明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。傑倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The guard's familiarity with the corridor helped reconstruct the route taken that night. The museum hierarchy had discouraged junior cleaners from questioning senior curators. The landlady of the nearby boarding house remembered an unusual delivery before dawn. Beyond it, the marsh looked lush and peaceful, concealing collapsed drainage channels. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  He had spent years trying to nurture survivors who were taught to mourn in silence. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 警衛對走廊的熟悉協助重建當晚路徑。 館內階級制度曾使基層清潔員不敢質疑高階館長。 附近寄宿屋的女房東記得黎明前一件異常送貨。 更遠處的沼澤看似草木茂盛而平靜，實際卻藏著崩毀的排水道。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 他多年來努力照顧那些被要求默默哀悼的倖存者。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A fair hiring process would nurture younger engineers rather than reward personal loyalty. Rain pressed against the windows, and the river threatened to overflow its banks. A cleaner had begun to overhear the order before the supervisor closed the door. The company's new report could refute a rumor only by presenting verifiable records. A gull circled the quay where a long-abandoned concert hall once hosted a symphony every spring. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 公平聘任程序能培育年輕工程師，而非獎勵個人忠誠。 雨水敲打窗戶，河水幾乎溢出河岸。 主管關門前，清潔員已無意間聽到那項命令。 公司新報告只能用可驗證紀錄反駁謠言。 一隻海鷗盤旋在碼頭上，附近那座廢棄音樂廳從前每年春天都會演奏交響樂。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The site superintendent admitted that he had approved a broken gate's temporary use. A recorded symphony played in the lobby, strangely calm beside the anxious witnesses. The infirmary nurse placed a thermometer beside an ill courier and offered him rest before questioning. The turmoil caused by the rising water had not prevented Amira from keeping her records in order. Volunteers prepared wholesome meals for interviewees who had missed the last bus. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 現場總監承認自己曾批准暫時使用損壞閘門。 大廳播放錄製的交響樂，與焦慮證人形成奇特反差。 醫務室護士把溫度計放在生病信差旁，讓他先休息再接受詢問。 河水上漲引發的混亂，仍沒有阻止奧拉把紀錄保存整齊。 志工為錯過末班車的受訪者準備營養均衡的餐點。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Maia collects paper birth records from a coastal hospital whose servers have already been erased.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅從伺服器已遭清除的海岸醫院收集紙本出生紀錄。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. An old nurse points to a handwritten amendment that changes a child's birthplace without changing the parents.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 年邁護理師指出一處手寫修正，把孩子的出生地改掉，父母姓名卻未變。


## CHAPTER 05｜The First Migration Ship｜第一艘移民船

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：870**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. The ancestral archive shows the route of the earliest migrant ship and a recorded stop at Earth. Maia had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the orbital immigration court, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。祖源檔案記錄最早移民船航線，其中包含地球停靠站。瑪雅工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在軌道移民法庭，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Maia asked Jalen to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑪雅請傑倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The company academy offered a practical course on emergency power rather than executive slogans. A false accusation against the warehouse team was withdrawn after the access records were checked. A banquet was taking place across the courtyard, where donors praised the war effort. The engineering laboratory had once tested fertilizer for the northern farms and water treatments for coastal fishery villages. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The riverside fishery requested a dedicated backup line to protect its refrigerated catch. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公司學院開設緊急供電實務課，而非宣傳主管口號。 查核門禁紀錄後，對倉庫團隊的不實指控遭撤回。 庭院對面正在舉行宴會，捐助者讚美戰爭。 工程實驗室從前為北方農田測試肥料，並替海岸漁村研究水處理，後來軍方承包商把它改成設備倉庫。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 河岸漁業要求專用備電線路，以保護冷藏漁獲。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Commissioner Voss insists the stop was a tourist visit, not evidence of origin. Maia felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。沃斯委員堅稱那只是觀光停靠，不足以證明發源地。瑪雅一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The inspection revealed a small fracture in the connector supporting the temporary generator. Technicians learned to harness surplus power without bypassing the required safety checks. Each operator wore headphones to hear control-room instructions over the storm warnings. The clinic reviewed its oxygen intake records to calculate emergency battery demand. In the courtyard, a troupe rehearsed a ceremonial dance called the Maiden's Promise, though its lyrics had been rewritten to glorify the war. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 檢查發現支撐臨時發電機的接頭有一道小裂縫。 技師學習利用剩餘電力，不繞過必要安全檢查。 每位操作員戴耳機，以在風暴警報聲中聽清控制室指示。 診所檢視氧氣攝入紀錄，計算緊急電池需求。 庭院裡，一個劇團排練名為〈少女的承諾〉的儀式舞蹈，但歌詞早被改成頌揚戰爭。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A young apprentice described an accusation against Malik: he had supposedly stolen a design for offshore heat collectors from the academy. The palace sent invitations for a ceremonial banquet that would introduce prospective buyers to the disputed collection. A resident asked for a referendum on the long-term public ownership of the canal land. Malik invited Yu into the old workshop where they had learned to refine copper anchors. The nurses could finally rejoice when the new clinic batteries passed their first test. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名年輕學徒說，馬利克據稱偷走學院的離岸熱能採集器設計。 王宮寄出儀式宴會邀請函，要把爭議藏品介紹給潛在買家。 一名居民要求針對運河土地的長期公共所有權舉辦公投。 馬利克帶陳宇進入舊工坊，他們以前在這裡學習精煉銅製媒介。 新診所電池通過首次測試後，護理師終於能高興起來。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Some shareholders began to resent the safety veto, calling it an obstacle to profit. The operating room remained serene after its generator switched smoothly to backup power. A neighborhood pastor gave a short sermon about caring for people before property. The board refused to shuffle expenses between accounts to make the trial look cheaper. A singer borrowed Yu's scarf and explained that her tenant family had been evicted when army engineers demanded their land. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 部分股東開始怨恨安全否決權，稱它阻礙獲利。 手術室的發電機順利切換備電後，室內依舊平靜。 社區牧師簡短講道，提醒人們應先照顧人，再談財產。 董事會拒絕在帳戶間調換支出，營造試辦更便宜的假象。 一位歌手借了陳宇的圍巾，說自己的租戶家庭因軍方工程師徵地而遭驅逐。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. The ancestral archive shows the route of the earliest migrant ship and a recorded stop at Earth.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 祖源檔案記錄最早移民船航線，其中包含地球停靠站。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Commissioner Voss insists the stop was a tourist visit, not evidence of origin.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 沃斯委員堅稱那只是觀光停靠，不足以證明發源地。


## CHAPTER 06｜An Objection from Mars｜火星的異議

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：830**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Martian citizens organize a hearing because the new Earth ruling threatens families spread across two planets. Maia saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。火星公民組織聽證，因為新裁定威脅跨兩顆星球生活的家庭。瑪雅明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Maia could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Jalen suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑪雅無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。傑倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Yu continued to allege that the entire investigation was a publicity stunt. The contractor had promised to beautify the riverside warehouse before its safety inspection. Maia corrected a caption that wrongly described the marsh canoe as royal property. The contract dispute reached its climax when the two shipping weights appeared together. Yu asked the auditor to extract the original invoice data from the server. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 陳宇繼續聲稱整場調查只是公關表演。 承包商曾承諾在安檢前美化河岸倉庫。 瑪拉更正一則錯把沼地獨木舟稱為王室財產的說明。 當兩張出貨重量表一起出現時，契約爭議達到高潮。 陳宇要求稽核員從伺服器擷取原始發票資料。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The court tries to separate relatives into legally human and legally unclassified categories. Jalen asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Maia agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。法庭竟試圖把親人分成法律上的人類與未分類者。傑倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑪雅也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An extracurricular engineering club helped sort community reports without handling confidential files. Their teacher was hospitable, offering water and a shady bench to witnesses who had traveled far. The usually jolly driver grew quiet when he recognized the private auction seal. Workers offered to mow the neglected path so elderly witnesses could reach the hall. A supervisor with a white mustache signed the original warehouse inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一個課外工程社團協助分類社區通報，但不碰機密檔案。 他們的老師十分好客，為遠道而來的證人準備飲水與陰涼長椅。 平日開朗的車夫認出私人拍賣印章時沉默了。 工人提議修剪荒廢小徑，方便年長證人到會堂。 一名留白鬍子的主管簽署了倉庫原始清冊。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A grandmother told him to stop nagging and gave him an ounce of sweet tea. The old cafeteria became an oasis for night workers exhausted by the investigation. One ounce of damaged insulation was sealed for laboratory comparison with the other samples. The missing battery modules might have been stored on the outskirts of the city. A visiting parliament observer requested that the public hearing remain open to workers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一位祖母要他別再嘮叨，遞給他一小杯甜茶。 舊餐廳成為調查期間疲憊夜班工人的休息綠洲。 一盎司受損絕緣材料被封存，供實驗室與其他樣本比對。 失蹤的電池模組可能存放在城市郊區。 一名國會觀察員要求公開聽證繼續向工人開放。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The nobleman's methods were ruthless but often lawful on paper: he delayed funding, demanded impossible permits, and threatened public employees with dismissal. A local sculptor offered a warehouse sketch that showed the original side entrance. The repair team preferred simplicity in emergency instructions over impressive technical language. Simultaneous inspections at both gates revealed that one crate could not have traveled as claimed. Public confidence fell into a slump when the palace refused to explain the disputed caption. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這名貴族的方法冷酷，但在紙面上常常合法：延後經費、索取不可能取得的許可，並以解雇威脅公務員。 一名在地雕塑家提供倉庫素描，顯示原有側門。 維修團隊偏好簡單的緊急指示，而非炫目的技術詞。 兩處大門同時檢查，顯示一只木箱不可能按所稱路線運送。 王宮拒絕解釋爭議圖說，公眾信心因此陷入低潮。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Martian citizens organize a hearing because the new Earth ruling threatens families spread across two planets.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 火星公民組織聽證，因為新裁定威脅跨兩顆星球生活的家庭。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The court tries to separate relatives into legally human and legally unclassified categories.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 法庭竟試圖把親人分成法律上的人類與未分類者。


## CHAPTER 07｜The Signature Older Than Flight｜比飛行更古老的簽名

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：848**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Maia refused to begin with the official conclusion. Maia identifies a treaty signed centuries before the signer’s stated date of birth. In the orbital immigration court, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑪雅拒絕從官方結論開始。瑪雅發現一份條約的簽名日期，比簽署者宣稱的出生日期早了數世紀。在軌道移民法庭，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Maia asked Jalen not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑪雅請傑倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The trust owned one acre of land beside the canal, but ownership did not erase public access. A wealthy bachelor on the board objected to being excluded from the safety committee. Employees organized a charity boxing exhibition to support families hurt by the outage. The disciplinary rules required an independent hearing before any worker could be fired. An emigrant engineer explained why the old contract translation was unclear to new workers. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 信託持有運河旁一英畝土地，但所有權不會抹去公共通行權。 董事會上一位富有的單身漢反對自己被排除在安全委員會外。 員工舉辦慈善拳擊表演，支援停電受影響的家庭。 紀律規則要求解雇任何工人前先有獨立聽證。 一名移居海外的工程師解釋舊合約翻譯為何讓新工人難懂。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The signature is authentic, but it belongs to a reused title rather than a single historical person. Maia watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Jalen recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。簽名是真的，簽署的卻是世襲職稱，不是某位歷史人物。瑪雅看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。傑倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Yu refused to equate a delayed audit with a failure of leadership. The young witness began to falter when Vask's lawyer asked three questions at once. Farmers argued that the fertility of the floodplain depended on seasonal access to both banks. Halda refused to forsake the elderly workers merely to recover the Tide Star sooner. A gay employee asked whether the revised benefits would cover his husband equally. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沉舟拒絕把稽核延誤等同領導失敗。 瓦斯克律師一次提出三個問題時，年輕證人開始結巴猶豫。 農民指出，沖積平原的土壤肥力仰賴兩岸季節性通行。 哈爾妲拒絕為了早點找回星盤而拋棄年長工人。 一名男同性戀員工詢問新福利是否同等保障他的丈夫。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: At the intersection of two estate paths, the gloom of approaching rain made the palace garden seem smaller. A survey found the dangerous intersection near the substation lacked proper lighting. The route had been damaged by a landslide years earlier and never fully repaired. The children needed warm food to nourish their bodies before another winter arrived. The orthodox court scholars disliked the river dialect but admitted its grammar was legally relevant. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 在莊園兩條小徑交叉處，將至的雨天陰鬱讓宮廷花園顯得更小。 調查發現變電站旁危險路口缺少適當照明。 該路線多年前遭山崩破壞，從未完全修復。 孩子們需要溫暖食物滋養身體，才能迎接下一個冬天。 正統宮廷學者不喜歡河語方言，卻承認其文法具有法律相關性。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The prohibition on unsafe work could not be canceled through a private annex. Yu asked for the provisional orders issued before the border fire; Yu said they had been destroyed in a flood. The vote was unanimous, yet the council could not erase an independent court order. The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. A woodpecker tapped the hostel roof while the workers discussed their missing wages. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 禁止危險作業的規定不能透過私人附約取消。 陳宇要求查看邊境大火前發布的臨時命令，陳宇卻說文件在洪水中毀了。 表決結果一致，議會卻無法抹除獨立法庭的命令。 總督的虛榮使他展示了一張大得無法近看細讀的地圖。 工人討論積欠薪資時，一隻啄木鳥敲著宿舍屋頂。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

| L6-0593 | `nourish(ment)` | v./(n.) | `nourish` | 滋養 | The children needed warm food to nourish their bodies before another winter arrived. |
| L6-0620 | `orthodox` | adj. | `orthodox` | 傳統正統的 | The orthodox court scholars disliked the river dialect but admitted its grammar was legally relevant. |
| L6-0701 | `prohibition` | n. | `prohibition` | 禁止 | The prohibition on unsafe work could not be canceled through a private annex. |
| L6-0709 | `provisional` | adj. | `provisional` | 臨時的 | Yu asked for the provisional orders issued before the border fire; Yu said they had been destroyed in a flood. |
| L6-0938 | `unanimous` | adj. | `unanimous` | 全體一致的 | The vote was unanimous, yet the council could not erase an independent court order. |
| L6-0957 | `vanity` | n. | `vanity` | 虛榮 | The governor's vanity had led him to display a larger map than anyone could read closely. |
| L6-0995 | `woodpecker` | n. | `woodpecker` | 啄木鳥 | A woodpecker tapped the hostel roof while the workers discussed their missing wages. |

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Maia identifies a treaty signed centuries before the signer’s stated date of birth.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅發現一份條約的簽名日期，比簽署者宣稱的出生日期早了數世紀。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The signature is authentic, but it belongs to a reused title rather than a single historical person.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 簽名是真的，簽署的卻是世襲職稱，不是某位歷史人物。


## CHAPTER 08｜The Quiet Archive Moon｜安靜的檔案月

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：870**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Maia and Jalen travel to an archive moon where records are preserved without centralized identity software. Maia asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。瑪雅與傑倫前往檔案衛星，當地不用中央身分軟體保存紀錄。瑪雅請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Maia listened longer than the schedule allowed. Jalen reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑪雅聽得比議程允許的更久。傑倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An accumulation of sediment had changed the waterways, and the official map was no longer accurate. A vendor offered each inspector a sealed beverage, but they declined gifts during the audit. The blond driver in the delivery photograph confirmed that he had used the wrong route. Lieutenant Yu sought clearance for the witnesses, while Malik asked a local surveyor to certify the paths through the marsh. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The warehouse manager demanded official clearance before the team opened the locked crates. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 累積的泥沙改變了水道，官方地圖已不準確。 供應商給每位檢查員密封飲料，但眾人在稽核期間拒絕收禮。 送貨照片裡的金髮司機證實自己走錯了路。 陳宇中尉替證人申請通行許可，馬利克則請地方測量員認證穿越濕地的路線。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 倉庫經理要求團隊開啟鎖箱前先取得正式許可。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. A curator admits that an earlier Earth delegation asked for certain files to remain sealed. Maia read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Jalen did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。館長承認，早年的地球使團曾要求封存部分檔案。瑪雅把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。傑倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A copy of those figures survived in the harbor office, where a careful official had refused to deduct unexplained losses. At noon, a provincial official presented a report declaring that all missing equipment had been destined for hospital repairs. The survey team had to exert care when lifting the old storage chest from damp floorboards. The porter rejected the heroic story that he had single-handedly saved the entire collection. Lieutenant Amira sought clearance for the witnesses, while Kes asked a local surveyor to certify the paths through the marsh. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 港務處保留了一份數字副本，一名謹慎的官員拒絕扣除來源不明的損失。 中午，一名省府官員提交報告，宣稱所有失蹤設備都注定要送去修理醫院。 測量團隊從潮濕地板抬起舊儲藏箱時必須格外用力且小心。 搬運員拒絕那個把他說成單槍匹馬救出全部收藏的英雄故事。 阿瑪雅中尉替證人申請通行許可，凱斯則請地方測量員認證穿越濕地的路線。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Literate workers could read the manual, but the team also prepared diagrams for everyone else. The provincial court stood beside a swamp where lotus flowers opened at dawn. The marginal note explained why the river verb described custody rather than ownership. The seals showed an overlap of ink from two different periods, prompting further laboratory tests. A provincial inspector confirmed that the classroom register had not left the district for ten years. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 識字的工人能閱讀手冊，團隊也替其他人準備圖解。 省級法庭位於沼澤旁，黎明時蓮花盛開。 頁邊註記解釋為何河語動詞指保管而非所有權。 印章顯示兩個年代的墨跡重疊，因此需要進一步化驗。 一名地方督察確認，教室登記冊十年間未離開該區。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The new technician's qualification was checked by an independent professional board. Volunteers brought refreshments to the residents waiting outside the warehouse. The team tried to retrieve a broken seal without disturbing the surrounding evidence. The clerk entered swamp in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The children learned that a translator's vocation involved responsibility to readers, not blind loyalty to rulers. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 新技師的資格由獨立專業委員會核對。 志工為倉庫外等待的居民送來茶點。 團隊試圖取回破損封條，同時不干擾周圍證物。 書記將「swamp」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 孩子們學到翻譯家的職志是對讀者負責，而非盲目效忠統治者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Maia and Jalen travel to an archive moon where records are preserved without centralized identity software.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑪雅與傑倫前往檔案衛星，當地不用中央身分軟體保存紀錄。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. A curator admits that an earlier Earth delegation asked for certain files to remain sealed.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 館長承認，早年的地球使團曾要求封存部分檔案。


## CHAPTER 09｜The Refugees in the Footnote｜註腳裡的難民

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：864**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The sealed files reveal that Earth secretly sheltered refugees from a war between older star empires. Maia checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。封存文件顯示，地球曾祕密收容來自古老星際帝國戰爭的難民。瑪雅核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Jalen arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Maia could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 傑倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑪雅感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The public statement might alienate frightened investors, but hiding the altered seal would be worse. A black blot on the purchase order concealed the first digit of a serial number. Residents shared chili soup while waiting for the procurement hearing to begin. The committee demanded continuity in clinic service even if the trust's leadership changed. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The cumulative delays had increased costs far beyond the original repair estimate. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 公開聲明可能疏遠害怕的投資人，但隱瞞改動封條更糟。 採購單上的黑墨漬蓋住序號第一個數字。 居民等候採購聽證開始時，分享辣椒湯。 委員會要求即使信託領導階層換人，診所服務也不能中斷。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 累積延誤讓成本遠超原始維修估算。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. A footnote names the refugees as ancestors of people now considered the purest approved citizens. For a long moment no one spoke. Maia understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Jalen broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。註腳點名那些難民是如今被視為最純正合格公民者的祖先。一時之間無人開口。瑪雅明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。傑倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Yu made a decisive choice to publish the damaged-cabinet test before the vote. No worker was dispensable merely because an executive feared an embarrassing question. A visitor wore jasmine perfume that matched the scent noted by the night guard. The river lodge provided temporary rooms for families displaced during repairs. A maple tree near the entrance helped the courier identify the delivery location. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 陳宇果斷決定在表決前公布受損櫃體測試。 不能因主管害怕尷尬提問，就把任何工人當成可有可無。 某位訪客使用茉莉香水，與夜班警衛記下的氣味一致。 河邊旅舍為維修期間搬離的家庭提供臨時房間。 入口旁的楓樹協助快遞員辨認送貨地點。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The next witness was Yu, an envoy from the eastern federation whom the Empire officially called a rebel mistress of spies. In the next room, people debated how to mobilize villagers for a public meeting without provoking soldiers. Yu paused to muse over how often public trust had been treated as private property. The whistleblower's persistence finally persuaded investigators to examine the old account. One organizer identified herself as a socialist who wanted shared ownership of water stations, while another preferred independent village councils. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 下一名證人是東方聯邦使者伊絲拉；帝國官方稱她是指揮間諜的叛軍女頭目。 隔壁房裡，人們討論如何動員村民出席公開集會，又不激怒士兵。 陳宇停下來沉思，公共信任究竟有多少次被當成私人財產。 吹哨者的堅持終於讓調查員檢查舊帳戶。 一名組織者自稱社會主義者，主張水站共有；另一人則偏好獨立村議會。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Employees had a chance to socialize after the hearing without being questioned by reporters. The access log covered a span of seven hours, with one unexplained gap near midnight. The author had been ordered to trek into the mountains and test the stellar collector before civilians were evacuated. A vigorous debate arose over whether the corrected map should be released immediately. Every emergency sensor needed a waterproof housing before the coming storm. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 聽證後，員工終於能不受記者盤問地社交。 門禁紀錄涵蓋七小時，其中午夜附近有一段無法解釋的空白。 作者奉命徒步進入山區，在平民撤離前測試恆星收集器。 對於是否立刻公開更正地圖，現場展開激烈辯論。 每個緊急感測器都必須在風暴來前裝上防水外殼。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The sealed files reveal that Earth secretly sheltered refugees from a war between older star empires.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 封存文件顯示，地球曾祕密收容來自古老星際帝國戰爭的難民。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. A footnote names the refugees as ancestors of people now considered the purest approved citizens.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 註腳點名那些難民是如今被視為最純正合格公民者的祖先。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Declaration of Non-Origin｜否認發源的宣言

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：852**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Maia discovers that an Earth council once signed a declaration denying its status as a human origin world. Maia knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。瑪雅發現地球議會曾簽署宣言，否認自身為人類發源地。瑪雅知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** The sealed declaration lay between Maia and Commissioner Voss beneath the orbital court's cold light. Its headline said Earth had denied being humanity's origin. Voss had quoted those words in every broadcast. Maia turned the sheet over and found the expiration clause, written in an older hand: this designation would remain only until the refugee ships were safe. The document was not an act of shame. It was a shield meant to stop an ancient empire from locating displaced families. Voss had used that temporary shield as a permanent weapon against their descendants. Behind Maia, a refugee witness recognized her ancestor's signature and began to cry. Maia asked the judge to read the reverse side before ruling on a single word of the front.

**繁中** 封存宣言躺在瑪雅與沃斯委員之間，軌道法庭的冷光照著文件。標題說地球曾否認自己是人類起源地，沃斯在每次廣播裡都引用這幾個字。瑪雅翻到背面，發現另一種更古老筆跡寫的到期條款：此指定只維持到難民船安全為止。那不是羞恥宣言，而是保護流亡家庭免遭古老帝國追蹤的盾牌。沃斯卻把暫時盾牌當成永久武器，對付難民的後裔。瑪雅身後，一名難民證人認出祖先簽名，哭了起來。她請法官在裁定正面任何一個字之前，先讀完背面。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The supplier sought a concession that would grant it control over the canal for a decade. A cynical investor said patients were useful only when their stories moved the market. The old procurement rule treated silence as consent by default, which the new board rejected. An emergency fund should not deplete the clinic budget merely to improve quarterly profit. Soldiers handed out sweet buns, and the fragrance of crushed flowers covered the smell of oil along the river. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 供應商尋求特許權，想控制運河十年。 一位憤世嫉俗的投資人說，病人故事只有能推動市場時才有用。 舊採購規則預設沉默即同意，新董事會予以否決。 緊急基金不該只為改善季度利潤就耗盡診所預算。 士兵發放甜麵包，壓碎花朵的香氣蓋住河邊油味。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The original document makes clear this was a temporary shield for refugees, not a permanent surrender of citizens' rights. Maia felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。原件清楚寫明，那只是保護難民的臨時安排，並非永久放棄公民權。瑪雅一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No official would be permitted to intrude on protected witness meetings without a court order. A former jockey now driving delivery vans recognized the unusual generator model. The clerk recorded the literal wording of the ownership claim without adding her opinion. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the stellar collector would deplete public confidence. The court could prosecute fraud only after evidence met the required standard. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有法院命令，任何官員都不得闖入受保護的證人會面。 一名曾當賽馬騎師的廂車司機認出了不尋常的發電機型號。 書記逐字記下所有權主張，沒有加入個人意見。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑恆星收集器會耗盡公眾信心。 證據達到法定標準後，法庭才能起訴詐欺。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The maintenance inspector checked for radioactive materials because an old sensor carried a misleading label. The chancellor's lawyer made a sharp retort about national morale, claiming that questioning the colony reactor would deplete public confidence. A young courier came forward with a screwdriver and a box of broken clock parts. Outside, an elderly vendor offered each departing witness a serving of warm soup. The tower's splendor seemed irrelevant beside the dark apartments across the river. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 維修檢查員因舊感測器標示誤導而檢查有無放射性物質。 宰相律師尖銳反駁，談起國民士氣，聲稱質疑殖民反應爐會耗盡公眾信心。 一名年輕信差帶著螺絲起子和一箱壞時鐘零件上前。 庭外，一名年長攤販給離開的每位證人一份熱湯。 相較河對岸漆黑的公寓，大樓的華麗顯得毫無意義。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A spontaneous murmur passed through the audience. A witness explained that her stepfather had worked on the first version of the old substation. The nervous clerk began to stutter before the judge invited her to speak slowly. The city agreed to subsidize emergency batteries for low-income patients during the trial. The military had claimed its newest heat reservoirs contained harmless synthetic stone. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 旁聽席傳出自發的低語。 一名證人解釋繼父曾參與舊變電站的初版工程。 緊張的書記開始口吃，法官請她慢慢說。 市府同意在試辦期間補助低收入病人的緊急電池。 軍方聲稱最新儲能庫採用無害的合成石材；學院在丟棄的隔熱材料中發現少量放射性礦物痕跡，但含量不足以確認疾病原因。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。
