### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Maia discovers that an Earth council once signed a declaration denying its status as a human origin world.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅發現地球議會曾簽署宣言，否認自身為人類發源地。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The original document makes clear this was a temporary shield for refugees, not a permanent surrender of citizens' rights.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 原件清楚寫明，那只是保護難民的臨時安排，並非永久放棄公民權。


## CHAPTER 11｜A Clause Without an End｜沒有期限的條款

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：819**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Voss invokes the old declaration as grounds for mass disenfranchisement, ignoring its expiration clause. Maia had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the orbital immigration court, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。沃斯援引舊宣言剝奪群眾公民權，卻忽略其中的到期條款。瑪雅工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在軌道移民法庭，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Maia could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Jalen suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑪雅無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。傑倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Yu brought a thermometer calibrated in Celsius and a probe designed to measure changes in river heat. Excessive friction inside the relay produced the unusual vibration recorded during the test. A stray dog began to growl when inspectors approached the abandoned loading dock. The lawyer said a forged instruction could induce workers to override a safe setting. The fishermen who inhabit these shores would need a permanent place on the safety board. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 瑪雅帶來以攝氏度校準的溫度計，以及測量河水熱能變化的探針。 繼電器內過度摩擦造成測試中記錄的異常震動。 檢查員靠近廢置裝貨區時，一隻流浪狗開始低吼。 律師說偽造指令可能誘使工人覆寫安全設定。 居住在這片海岸的漁民需要在安全委員會中擁有永久席次。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Maia finds two original witnesses who can testify about the treaty's limited purpose. Jalen asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Maia agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。瑪雅找到兩位原始證人，能說明條約有限的目的。傑倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑪雅也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: It was ironic that the trust's security account had been active long after its owner died. A retired mechanic lived on a grassy mound near the station. A peacock from a nearby garden wandered close to the temporary generator fence. A pianist played for patients while the clinic's backup system was tested. Amira brought a thermometer calibrated in Celsius and a probe designed to measure changes in river heat. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 諷刺的是，信託保全帳戶在原持有人死後很久仍活躍。 一名退休技師住在測站附近的草丘上。 附近花園的一隻孔雀走近臨時發電機圍欄。 診所測試備電系統時，一位鋼琴家為病人演奏。 瑪雅帶來以攝氏度校準的溫度計，以及測量河水熱能變化的探針。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The respective duties of the city and company had been confused in the old emergency plan. Yu admitted that her previous shortsighted policy had made a second approval optional. The nurse asked the engineers to simplify the battery instructions for home patients. A vessel crossing the strait was delayed by the same storm approaching the city. The superficial inspection had missed heat damage deep inside the relay cabinet. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 舊緊急計畫混淆了市府與公司的各自責任。 陳宇承認過去短視的政策使第二道核准變成可選。 護士請工程師簡化居家病人的電池使用說明。 一艘穿越海峽的船因同場暴風雨而延誤。 表面的檢查漏掉了繼電器櫃內部深處的熱損傷。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No official could suppress a valid correction merely because it embarrassed the palace. The symmetry of the instruments mattered because each team had to measure the same change independently. An expired access token had been used to send the dormant account's last message. The public committee tried to unify the three separate emergency notification systems. The nurse emphasized the urgency of restoring the clinic's refrigerated medical supply. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 官員不能只因更正令王室難堪便壓下有效資料。 儀器的對稱配置很重要，因為每組人都必須獨立測量同一項變化。 最後一封休眠帳戶訊息使用了一枚過期存取憑證。 公共委員會試圖整合三套分離的緊急通知系統。 護士強調恢復診所冷藏醫療用品供應的急迫性。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Voss invokes the old declaration as grounds for mass disenfranchisement, ignoring its expiration clause.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 沃斯援引舊宣言剝奪群眾公民權，卻忽略其中的到期條款。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Maia finds two original witnesses who can testify about the treaty's limited purpose.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑪雅找到兩位原始證人，能說明條約有限的目的。


## CHAPTER 12｜The Planet of No Witnesses｜沒有證人的星球

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：860**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. The immigration court claims the witnesses have died, though both still serve in an ancient orbital archive. Maia saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。移民法庭宣稱證人都已死亡，兩人卻仍在古老軌道檔案館任職。瑪雅明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Maia asked Jalen not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑪雅請傑倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A small brook flowed behind the pump house and concealed part of its old foundation. The canal rescue team removed fragile coral samples from a science display before flooding. Yu refused to deprive residents of access while the lease remained in dispute. Workers used a platform to elevate the backup batteries above the projected flood line. The lease had an expiration date, but no one could find its required public notice. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 抽水站後方的小溪遮住一部分舊地基。 洪水前，運河救援隊移走科學展示裡脆弱的珊瑚樣本。 租約仍有爭議時，陳宇拒絕剝奪居民的通行權。 工人用平台把備用電池抬高到預測淹水線以上。 租約有到期日，卻沒有人找得到法定公開通知。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Their prolonged lives raise questions, but their dated testimony can be checked against independent recordings. Maia watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Jalen recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。他們異常長壽引人質疑，但帶日期的證詞仍可用獨立錄音查核。瑪雅看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。傑倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The retired mechanic's failing eyesight made written testimony easier than reading small diagrams. A volunteer offered a hearty meal to the crews who remained at the canal overnight. A rescue bird seemed to hover above the water gate as the current rose. The inland authorities had demanded fees for boats using an island landing they did not own. The lawyer's insistence on public notice changed the entire lease negotiation. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 退休技師視力衰退，書面作證比閱讀小圖更容易。 志工給留守運河的工班一頓豐盛餐點。 水位上漲時，一隻救援鳥似乎在水閘上方盤旋。 內陸當局曾向使用其無所有權島岸碼頭的船隻收費。 律師堅持公開通知，改變了整場租約談判。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The temporary lease allowed access without transferring ownership of the pump house. A visiting legislator asked whether the emergency access rule was enforceable after the trial. The majestic executive tower looked small from the muddy work site beside the canal. The new gate gained momentum as the winch began pulling it slowly upward. The monarch had signed the original treaty but not the later pamphlet sent to the island. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 臨時租約允許通行，但不轉讓抽水站所有權。 來訪議員詢問試辦結束後，緊急通行規則是否仍可執行。 從運河旁泥濘工地望去，宏偉的總部大樓也顯得渺小。 絞盤慢慢往上拉時，新閘門逐漸有了動能。 君主簽署過原始條約，卻未簽署送往島上的後來小冊子。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: An animal shelter asked for reliable heating to protect newborn offspring during power cuts. A petrol generator was retained as a backup only after its emissions were tested. The operator had to rotate the valve slowly to prevent a sudden pressure change. Maelin warned that the false fee might strangle the island's small repair trade over time. The medical box contained a surgical instrument whose owner had never consented to its sale. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 動物收容所要求可靠暖氣，以保護太陽衰減期間剛出生的幼獸。 汽油發電機通過排放測試後才被保留作備用。 操作員必須緩慢旋轉閥門，以免壓力突然變化。 瑪雅警告，不實收費長期可能扼殺島上小型修船業。 醫療箱中有一件手術器械，原主人從未同意出售。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The immigration court claims the witnesses have died, though both still serve in an ancient orbital archive.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 移民法庭宣稱證人都已死亡，兩人卻仍在古老軌道檔案館任職。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Their prolonged lives raise questions, but their dated testimony can be checked against independent recordings.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 他們異常長壽引人質疑，但帶日期的證詞仍可用獨立錄音查核。


## CHAPTER 13｜The Price of a Definition｜定義的代價

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：858**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Maia refused to begin with the official conclusion. Maia meets families who have lost employment and medicine under the new definition of humanity. In the orbital immigration court, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑪雅拒絕從官方結論開始。瑪雅遇見因新版人類定義失去工作與藥品的家庭。在軌道移民法庭，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Maia listened longer than the schedule allowed. Jalen reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑪雅聽得比議程允許的更久。傑倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An accountant used basic algebra to explain how the reserve funds had been divided. The technician said the antonym of 'authorized' was not automatically 'criminal'. The team learned to conserve battery power during long evidence collection sessions. The librarian was an outgoing woman who kept a discreet inventory of each volume sent for repair. A servant brought dressing for a simple supper salad, then apologized for interrupting the investigation. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 會計師用基礎代數說明準備金如何被拆分。 技師說「獲授權」的反義詞不會自動等於「犯罪」。 團隊在長時間蒐證時學會節約電池電力。 圖書管理員性格外向，卻謹慎記錄每本送修書籍的清單。 一名侍者送來簡單晚餐沙拉的醬汁，為打斷調查道歉。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. An emergency judge rules that basic care must continue while citizenship is contested. Maia read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Jalen did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。緊急法官裁定，公民資格爭議期間仍須持續提供基本照護。瑪雅把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。傑倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: At dusk, they examined a sequence of small drawings printed beside the equations. The school's encyclopedia gave a historical example of the river word used for temporary safekeeping. The memory of her own careless signature would haunt Maelin even if the tribunal cleared her of treason. The former curator's indifference had allowed incorrect names to remain on the wall. The clerk entered inventory in the testimony without deciding what it proved. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 黃昏時，他們檢查方程式旁的一連串小圖。 學校百科全書提供河語該詞表示暫時保管的歷史例句。 即使法庭洗清叛國罪，自己草率簽名的記憶仍會縈繞瑪雅。 前館長的冷漠讓錯誤姓名長期留在牆上。 書記將「inventory」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: A mother sang a lullaby in the waiting room while investigators examined the scanner files. The witness used the term outgoing in the report, but the team checked the actual event instead of relying on labels. At the community meeting, former pupils outnumber the museum officials by three to one. The outward appearance of the document was convincing, but its metadata was inconsistent. A court officer had to restrain an angry spectator who rushed toward the translation table. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 調查員檢查掃描檔時，一名母親在候診室唱搖籃曲。 證人在報告中使用「outgoing」一詞，團隊仍然核查真實事件，而非只依賴標籤。 社群會議中，舊學生人數是館方官員的三倍。 文件外觀令人信服，後設資料卻不一致。 一名法庭官員不得不攔住衝向翻譯桌的憤怒旁聽者。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: One illustrated page honored a sportswoman whose sportsmanship had helped rival villages organize a shared winter race. The team praised the workers' sportsmanship during a charity tournament held after the shift. The assistant left her suitcase with security before entering the protected interview room. The judge issued a tentative finding that the word meant custody, subject to expert review. The review was underway before the palace press office could issue another false summary. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 其中一頁插圖表彰一位女運動員，她展現的運動家精神曾協助敵對村莊合辦冬季賽事。 輪班後舉辦慈善賽，團隊讚賞工人的運動家精神。 助理進入受保護訪談室前，把行李箱交給保全。 法官提出暫時認定：該詞意為保管，但仍須專家複查。 王宮新聞室再次發布錯誤摘要前，審查已經展開。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Maia meets families who have lost employment and medicine under the new definition of humanity.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅遇見因新版人類定義失去工作與藥品的家庭。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. An emergency judge rules that basic care must continue while citizenship is contested.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 緊急法官裁定，公民資格爭議期間仍須持續提供基本照護。


## CHAPTER 14｜What the Genome Cannot Prove｜基因無法證明的事

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：855**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Independent geneticists demonstrate that no single birthplace can establish moral or civic worth. Maia asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。獨立遺傳學者證明，沒有一個出生地足以決定道德或公民價值。瑪雅請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Jalen arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Maia could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 傑倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑪雅感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The admiral had inquired about the change and received an instruction bearing the chancellor's seal. The new floor plan might lead rescuers astray if it omitted the hidden stair. The workers' brotherhood was built through shared safety practices rather than blind loyalty. A clinic asked the power team to protect refrigeration for patients with diabetes. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The examiner explained how to differentiate negligence from deliberate interference. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 上將曾詢問為何改道，得到一份蓋有宰相印的指令。 新平面圖若漏掉隱藏樓梯，可能讓救援者走錯路。 工人間的兄弟情誼建立在共同安全實踐，而非盲目忠誠。 診所要求供電團隊保護糖尿病患者相關冷藏設備。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 審查員解釋如何區分疏忽與蓄意干擾。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. The court agrees to hear the case as a matter of rights rather than ancestral prestige. For a long moment no one spoke. Maia understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Jalen broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。法院同意以權利爭議而非祖先榮耀審理案件。一時之間無人開口。瑪雅明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。傑倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A later message told him that questioning it would bring doom upon his command. At the harbor offices, workers were calculating the excess fuel the fleet had consumed during the false emergency. The judges considered it feasible to secure the documents without exposing workers to the flooded passage. A feminine style of handwriting was noted in one margin, but Maelin warned against guessing the writer's identity. It was imperative to preserve the original file before examining any copies. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 後來另有消息威脅，若再質疑，整支艦隊便會遭厄運。 港口辦公室裡，工人正計算艦隊在假緊急情況下消耗的超額燃料。 法官認為可以在不讓工人冒險進入淹水通道的情況下保全文件。 一處頁邊字跡看似女性筆法，瑪雅卻警告不可據此猜作者身分。 檢查任何副本前，保存原始檔至關重要。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The panel warned that a hasty verdict could inflict harm on innocent employees. Halda asked the contractor to inquire whether his company had kept duplicate keys. An archival photograph showed a clinic that had once offered psychotherapy to displaced families. Keir measured the radius of the old harbor turning circle to check whether the delivery wagon could pass. The chancellor's reign over the records office had lasted longer than any clerk could remember. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 小組警告倉促判決可能傷害無辜員工。 哈爾妲請承包商查詢公司是否保留備鑰。 檔案照片顯示一間曾替流離家庭提供心理治療的診所。 凱爾測量舊港迴轉圈半徑，確認運貨車能否通過。 宰相掌管紀錄辦公室的時期長到書記們都記不得起點。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The audit identified a serious shortcoming in the emergency bypass approval process. Yu told the workers to tuck loose cables safely away from the moving gate. The underpass near the canal would close whenever the flood warning reached the red level. Only one licensed vessel was recorded at the wharf on the night in question. Iva described the woe caused by a border rule that treated her neighbors' homes as cargo. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 稽核指出緊急繞過授權流程有一項嚴重缺陷。 陳宇叫工人把鬆散電線收妥，遠離活動閘門。 運河旁地下通道在洪水警報達紅色時必須封閉。 案發夜碼頭只記錄了一艘有執照船舶。 伊娃描述邊界規定把鄰居家園當貨物所造成的悲苦。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Independent geneticists demonstrate that no single birthplace can establish moral or civic worth.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 獨立遺傳學者證明，沒有一個出生地足以決定道德或公民價值。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The court agrees to hear the case as a matter of rights rather than ancestral prestige.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 法院同意以權利爭議而非祖先榮耀審理案件。


## CHAPTER 15｜A History with Two Authors｜兩名作者的歷史

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：805**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. Maia learns that the sealed declaration was drafted jointly by Earth and star refugees to prevent mass deportation. Maia checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。瑪雅得知封存宣言由地球與星際難民共同擬定，為的是避免大規模遣返。瑪雅核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Maia asked Jalen to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑪雅請傑倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An overseas affiliate had received part of the disputed maintenance payment. Yu said she would always aspire to build a company that could survive criticism. The canal district stood on the brink of another dangerous flood when the warning arrived. A ceramic insulator cracked during a preliminary test and was immediately replaced. Workers began a quiet chant demanding their overdue wages outside the hearing room. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一家海外關係企業收到部分有爭議的維修款。 陳宇說她會一直渴望建立一家能承受批評的公司。 警報送達時，運河區正處於另一場危險洪水的邊緣。 一個陶瓷絕緣子在初步測試時裂開，立刻被替換。 工人在聽證室外低聲齊喊要求支付欠薪。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. Voss's office has displayed only the paragraph that makes Earth look illegitimate. Maia felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。沃斯辦公室只展示讓地球看似不合法的段落。瑪雅一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A science museum's chimpanzee exhibit reminded visiting students that cleverness is not accountability. The staff received a meal coupon instead of the full wages they were owed. An elaborate publicity event would be a distraction from the incomplete engineering test. The independent examiner asked for two separate witnesses to verify the transfer signature. Yu had an instinctive urge to blame her uncle, but she waited for evidence. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 科學博物館的黑猩猩展示提醒學生，聰明不等於負責。 員工拿到餐券，卻沒有領到應得的全部薪水。 華麗的公關活動只會分散對未完成工程測試的注意。 獨立審查員要求兩名分開的證人驗證轉帳簽名。 陳宇本能地想責怪叔父，卻選擇等待證據。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Rare wooden frames would perish if the heating system failed during a damp winter. The committee would ratify the new timetable only after the engineers approved its safety threshold. An investor's reckless demand for immediate reopening was rejected by the worker committee. The laboratory could reproduce the relay failure under controlled conditions. A retired sergeant helped volunteers practice crowd control without using unnecessary force. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 潮濕冬天若供暖系統故障，珍貴木框可能毀壞。 工程師確認安全門檻後，委員會才會正式批准新時程。 一名投資人魯莽要求立即重啟，遭工人委員會拒絕。 實驗室可在受控條件下重現繼電器故障。 一名退役中士協助志工練習人群管理，不使用多餘武力。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sparrow settled on the office railing while Yu reviewed the storm map. A newspaper subscription paid for by the trust had been used to circulate misleading reports. Thereafter, any extension of the temporary order would require another public review. The company funded vocational training for workers who needed new equipment qualifications. A community yoga class moved indoors when the wind warning was raised. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 陳宇檢視風暴地圖時，一隻麻雀停在辦公室欄杆上。 信託支付的一份報紙訂閱被用來傳播誤導報導。 此後，任何延長臨時命令的決定都必須再經公開複查。 公司資助需要新設備資格的工人接受職業訓練。 風警升級時，一堂社區瑜伽課改到室內。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. Maia learns that the sealed declaration was drafted jointly by Earth and star refugees to prevent mass deportation.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑪雅得知封存宣言由地球與星際難民共同擬定，為的是避免大規模遣返。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Voss's office has displayed only the paragraph that makes Earth look illegitimate.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 沃斯辦公室只展示讓地球看似不合法的段落。


## CHAPTER 16｜The Census of Absences｜缺席者人口普查

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：832**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Jalen organizes a parallel census recording those erased from official identity databases. Maia knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。傑倫組織平行人口普查，記錄被官方身分資料庫刪除的人。瑪雅知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Maia could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Jalen suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑪雅無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。傑倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A volunteer with elegant calligraphy labeled boxes holding the workers' emergency records. A porter helped move heavy medical boxes to the shelter, their cartons packed beneath cardboard covers. The clerk entered carton in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The community kitchen agreed to cater for night crews but requested accurate meal counts. A patient needed medication to lower cholesterol, and the clinic protected its refrigerated stock. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一名書法漂亮的志工為裝有工人緊急紀錄的箱子寫標籤。 一名搬運工協助把沉重醫療箱送到避難所，紙箱外包著硬紙板。 書記將「carton」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 社區廚房同意供應夜班餐食，但要求準確計算份數。 一名病人需要降膽固醇藥物，診所保護了冷藏藥品庫存。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. The count reveals that many non-Earth citizens have also been stripped of rights for political reasons. Jalen asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Maia agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。統計顯示，許多非地球公民也因政治理由被剝奪權利。傑倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑪雅也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Two nights after the hearing, a disturbance swept through the central heat network. Warning lamps flickered and several reservoirs began to cool downward toward dangerous levels. Residents were indignant that a broken siren had not been repaired after earlier complaints. One apprentice began to marvel that a child could have caused so much damage, but the others reminded him to examine the metal first. An independent expert agreed to mediate the dispute over delivery priorities. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 聽證兩夜後，中央熱能網發生異常。 警示燈忽明忽暗，數座儲能庫的溫度一路下降到危險範圍。 居民對警報器在多次申訴後仍未修復感到憤慨。 一名學徒驚嘆連孩子都可能造成如此破壞，其他人提醒他先檢查金屬。 一名獨立專家同意調解送貨優先順序爭議。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The backup system's operation seemed miraculous, but Yu insisted on measuring its limits. Volunteers used a paddle to move supplies across a shallow flooded section. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  There was nothing miraculous about the effort, only preparation and persistent work. The clerk entered porter in the testimony without deciding what it proved. The clinic could purify emergency water without relying on the damaged central pump. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 備電系統的運作看似奇蹟，陳宇仍堅持測量其限制。 志工用船槳划過淺淹水區運送物資。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 這番努力毫無神蹟可言，只有事前準備和持續工作。 書記將「porter」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 診所可以淨化緊急用水，不必依賴損壞的中央抽水機。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Yu arrived with a training team that had rehearsed a safe shutdown at the academy. Technicians immediately shut the valves, but a relay cable had been cut. An orderly supply of stationery helped volunteers label and track every sealed package. A superstitious driver hesitated at the old tunnel, but followed the approved route. Inside, a broken walnut-colored handle had jammed the manual switch. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 瑪雅帶著曾在學院排練安全停機的訓練隊抵達。 技師立刻關閉閥門，但一條中繼電纜已被切斷。 文具供應有序，協助志工標記追蹤每份封存物品。 一位迷信的司機在舊隧道前猶豫，仍依核准路線行駛。 裡面一個胡桃木色的手柄卡住手動開關。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Jalen organizes a parallel census recording those erased from official identity databases.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 傑倫組織平行人口普查，記錄被官方身分資料庫刪除的人。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The count reveals that many non-Earth citizens have also been stripped of rights for political reasons.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 統計顯示，許多非地球公民也因政治理由被剝奪權利。


## CHAPTER 17｜The Witness Who Betrayed Earth｜背叛地球的證人

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：855**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. A former Earth envoy admits voting for secrecy to protect refugees, although the measure harmed later generations. Maia had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the orbital immigration court, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。前地球特使承認曾投票支持保密以保護難民，卻也傷害了後代。瑪雅工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在軌道移民法庭，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Maia asked Jalen not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑪雅請傑倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An analytical report separated the storm's direct damage from earlier maintenance failures. Residents discussed a boycott of the contractor but still demanded a fair evidence review. The inspector reported no casualty during the controlled load transfer, though several homes lost power. A nurse offered consolation to a family whose refrigerator had failed again. The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  The missing correspondence was recovered from the carton with the false bottom. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 一份分析報告將風暴直接損失與先前維修失誤分開。 居民討論抵制承包商，仍要求公平審查證據。 檢查員報告受控負載轉移無人傷亡，雖然幾戶太陽衰減。 一名護士安慰冰箱再次故障的家庭。 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 失蹤往來書信從有假底的紙箱中找回。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Maia refuses to turn his complicated choice into proof that all Earth citizens consented. Maia watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Jalen recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。瑪雅拒絕把特使複雜的選擇當成全體地球公民曾同意的證據。瑪雅看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。傑倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: At a refugee center, volunteers were cultivating vegetables in a courtyard once used for weapons storage. The company agreed to disclosure of past maintenance failures under an independent protocol. The curator rejected a donor's demand that his name remain on the display for eternity. An operator feared he would flunk the new training test, so the team scheduled practice. A nurse explained new hygiene rules after several children caught a seasonal illness. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一處難民中心的志工正在曾存放武器的院子裡種菜。 公司同意依獨立程序揭露過去維修失誤。 館長拒絕捐助者要求名字永遠留在展示上的條件。 一名操作員擔心新訓練測試不及格，團隊安排練習。 護理師解釋新的衛生規定，因為幾名孩子感染季節性疾病。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The nurse hoped that patient explanations would kindle trust among families who had long feared officials. Yu listened while workers debated preventive measures and the layout of sleeping quarters. The recovered reserve might be lucrative for investors, but its first duty was public repair. The outward sign of the guild looked respectable, although its purchasing records required close examination. Amira listened while workers debated preventive measures and the layout of sleeping quarters. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 護理師希望耐心的解說能在長年害怕官員的家庭之間重新點燃信任。 陳宇聽著工人討論預防措施與寢室布局。 追回準備金可能讓投資人獲利，但首要用途是公共修復。 公會外部招牌看來體面，但採購紀錄仍需嚴格審查。 阿阿瑪雅聽著工人討論預防措施與寢室布局。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A child gave a shriek when the temporary siren sounded during a supervised test. The old courier began to sneeze from dust, and the court permitted a short break. The team measured electrical stimulation only on equipment designed to receive it. The repeated verification was tiresome, but the witnesses preferred it to another silent mistake. The technician had to wrestle with a rusted cabinet door before opening it safely. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 受監督的測試中，暫時警報器響起時，一名孩子尖叫。 老信差因灰塵開始打噴嚏，法庭准許短暫休息。 團隊只對設計可接受電刺激的設備測量該項數值。 反覆核驗令人疲倦，證人卻寧願如此，也不願再有默默發生的錯誤。 技師必須費力對付生鏽櫃門，才得以安全打開。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A former Earth envoy admits voting for secrecy to protect refugees, although the measure harmed later generations.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 前地球特使承認曾投票支持保密以保護難民，卻也傷害了後代。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. Maia refuses to turn his complicated choice into proof that all Earth citizens consented.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑪雅拒絕把特使複雜的選擇當成全體地球公民曾同意的證據。


## CHAPTER 18｜The Court That Tried to Close｜企圖關門的法庭

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：846**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Voss tries to end the hearing through an emergency jurisdiction rule. Maia saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。沃斯試圖利用緊急管轄規定終止聽證。瑪雅明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Maia listened longer than the schedule allowed. Jalen reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑪雅聽得比議程允許的更久。傑倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The guard waited awhile before reporting the broken lock because he feared punishment. The new policy would broaden independent access to maintenance records without exposing private data. A volunteer from the western cape had traveled overnight to support flood relief. A captive audience at the shareholder meeting deserved more than a polished speech. The clerk mistook a consonant in the old access code and had to check the original. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 警衛因害怕處罰，等了一段時間才通報壞鎖。 新政策將擴大獨立調閱維修紀錄的權限，不曝光個資。 一名西岬志工連夜趕來支援救災。 股東會上無法離席的聽眾值得聽到的不只是漂亮演說。 書記看錯舊門禁代碼的一個子音字母，只好查核原件。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Three judges discover the rule has expired and order public access to the evidence. Maia read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Jalen did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。三名法官發現規則早已失效，下令公開證據。瑪雅把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。傑倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: He tried to dictate which records could be copied and demanded the arrest of the detained key holder. A dwarf tree in the courtyard concealed a view of the server-room window. The museum released an excerpt of the court order while protecting private addresses. An intruder had moved the false partition without signing the vault access log. A mischievous intern joked about ghosts in the server room before seeing the serious evidence. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 他試圖指定哪些紀錄可以複製，並要求逮捕被拘留的鑰匙保管人。 庭院一棵矮樹擋住了伺服器室窗戶的視線。 博物館公開法庭命令節錄，同時保護私人地址。 闖入者未簽密庫出入簿便移動假隔板。 一名調皮實習生看到嚴肅證據前，還開玩笑說伺服器室有鬼。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: After the storm weakened, an operative from the chancellor's office arrived claiming authority over the warehouse. The witness said the interrogation had been an ordeal but chose to finish her testimony. The new charter would outlaw secret transfers of contested artifacts without a public register. A falling splinter could pinch a worker against the doorframe, so carpenters inspected the braces before anyone returned. A verified identification had to precede any announcement that the Tide Star was recovered. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 風暴稍緩後，宰相辦公室的一名特派員到場，自稱有權接管倉庫。 證人說詢問過程是一場煎熬，仍選擇完成證詞。 新章程將禁止未經公開登記便祕密轉移爭議文物。 掉落的木片可能把工人夾在門框旁，因此木匠先檢查支架，才讓人返回。 宣布潮星儀尋回前，必須先完成可靠辨識。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: Restoration of the locked server room required both a physical repair and new access rules. A safety statute required emergency doors to open even during electrical failure. The weather office warned that a tornado was possible far outside the city's service region. Wholesale food traders agreed to send supplies without demanding that residents sign the disputed land forms. Keir used a magnifying lens to zoom in on the faded serial number of a harbor gate. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 修復上鎖的伺服器室需要實體維修與新門禁規則。 一項安全法規要求緊急門即使停電也必須能開啟。 氣象單位警告城市服務範圍之外可能出現龍捲風。 批發食品商同意供貨，不要求居民簽署有爭議的土地文件。 凱爾用放大鏡放大查看港門褪色的序號。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. Voss tries to end the hearing through an emergency jurisdiction rule.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 沃斯試圖利用緊急管轄規定終止聽證。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Three judges discover the rule has expired and order public access to the evidence.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 三名法官發現規則早已失效，下令公開證據。


## CHAPTER 19｜A Planet on the Screen｜螢幕上的地球

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：846**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Maia refused to begin with the official conclusion. Earth residents speak to the court from schools, farms, hospitals, and repair shops. In the orbital immigration court, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑪雅拒絕從官方結論開始。地球居民從學校、農場、醫院與修理店對法庭發言。在軌道移民法庭，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Jalen arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Maia could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 傑倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑪雅感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The city agreed to fortify the canal barrier before the next flood season. A former clerk met the investigators at the pier, wearing a glamorous coat that looked out of place beside the weathered docks. Investigators could infer a possible route from the door logs but not the intruder's identity. The witness's intonation changed when the lawyer mentioned the dormant account. A hurried announcement would invariably miss some facts still being verified. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 市府同意在下個汛期前加固運河屏障。 一名退休書記在碼頭迎接調查員，穿著與風化碼頭格格不入的華麗外套。 調查員可從門禁紀錄推論可能路線，卻不能推斷闖入者身分。 律師提到休眠帳戶時，證人的語調改變了。 倉促公告總會漏掉一些仍在查證的事實。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Their ordinary lives undermine the claim that a government label can decide who belongs to humanity. For a long moment no one spoke. Maia understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Jalen broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。他們的日常生活瓦解了政府標籤能決定誰屬於人類的說法。一時之間無人開口。瑪雅明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。傑倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A courier's boat had stopped on a small isle during the disputed delivery period. Yu found it difficult to meditate while waiting for the independent report. A migrant boatbuilder identified the special knot used to seal the disputed package. The court reminded the crowd that every witness was mortal and could make mistakes. A nude figure in the old office mural had no connection with the damaged server. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 爭議送貨期間，快遞員的船曾停在小島上。 一名移工說，欠薪使自己難以反對主管。 一名外來移居的造船匠辨認出爭議包裹上的特殊繩結。 法庭提醒群眾，每名證人都是凡人，都可能犯錯。 舊辦公室壁畫中的裸體人像與損壞伺服器毫無關係。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The witness took an oath to tell the truth before answering questions about the purchase orders. An unfamiliar odor on the wrapping suggested that the crate had been stored near ship fuel. The residents called it outrageous that public safety had been used to manipulate asset prices. The air-quality team checked ozone levels near the damaged electrical equipment. Maia sealed the anonymous packet before asking an independent examiner to check its origin. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 證人在回答採購單問題前宣誓據實陳述。 包布上的陌生氣味顯示木箱可能曾放在船用燃料附近。 居民認為拿公共安全操弄資產價格實在令人髮指。 空氣品質團隊檢查受損電力設備附近的臭氧濃度。 瑪拉封存匿名文件包，再請獨立鑑定人查來源。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The new training required proficiency in both emergency shutdown and public communication. Yu refused to answer the whistleblower with scorn merely because his story changed. Investigators found no evidence that the courier had tried to smuggle batteries across the river. Halda crossed the hearing hall with a confident stride and placed the drawings before the judges. A trace of vanilla oil on the packing cloth linked two shipments from the same supplier. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 新訓練要求熟練緊急停機與公共溝通。 陳宇拒絕因吹哨者說法改變就輕蔑回應。 調查員沒發現快遞員試圖走私電池渡河的證據。 哈爾妲大步走過會堂，將描圖放到法官面前。 包裝布上的一絲香草油，把兩批貨物連到同一供應商。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. Earth residents speak to the court from schools, farms, hospitals, and repair shops.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 地球居民從學校、農場、醫院與修理店對法庭發言。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Their ordinary lives undermine the claim that a government label can decide who belongs to humanity.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 他們的日常生活瓦解了政府標籤能決定誰屬於人類的說法。


## CHAPTER 20｜The Commissioner’s List｜委員的名單

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：839**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** A messenger arrived before the bells had finished ringing. The news sounded impossible only because everyone had been trained not to question the institution that delivered it. Maia finds payments linking Voss's office to companies bidding on the properties of newly stateless people. Maia asked the messenger to repeat the part that had been omitted from the written notice. It was there, in the omission, that the day's real story began.

**繁中** 鐘聲尚未結束，信使已經抵達。消息之所以聽來不可能，只因每個人都習慣不質疑傳遞消息的制度。瑪雅發現沃斯辦公室收受款項，來源是競標無國籍者財產的公司。瑪雅請信使重述書面公告遺漏的部分。這一天真正的故事，就從那處缺漏開始。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Maia asked Jalen to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑪雅請傑倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The engineers followed the shutdown rule in accordance with the independent safety plan. The clinic kept the lights on for a counseling session about adolescence during the reduction. A caterpillar crawled across the emergency map while volunteers worked outdoors. Yu turned the backup valve clockwise according to the printed instructions. The rescue team used a compass when the floodwater covered familiar street markers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 工程師依獨立安全計畫遵守停機規則。 降載期間，診所仍維持一場青春期諮詢課的照明。 志工在室外工作時，一隻毛毛蟲爬過緊急地圖。 陳宇按照印刷指示順時針轉動備用閥門。 洪水淹沒熟悉的路標時，救援隊使用指南針。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The evidence prompts a separate corruption investigation without making the citizenship case depend on it. Maia felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。證據促成獨立貪腐調查，公民權訴訟則不必依附其結果。瑪雅一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: The second relay reading did not contradict the first, but showed a dangerous delay. Maia's claim of perfect safety was dubious after the failed warning siren. The ministry had used false figures to enhance its political standing as well as its budget. Chancellor Varr's lawyer raised a dubious objection, arguing that a monarch could exempt his own office from scrutiny. Outside the hall, soldiers stood beside a field where horses grazed. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 第二次繼電器讀數並未與第一次矛盾，而是顯示危險延遲。 警報器失靈後，廖梅宣稱完全安全的說法十分可疑。 部門曾利用錯誤數字提高政治地位，同時增加預算。 瓦爾律師提出可疑的異議，主張君主可以豁免自己的辦公室接受審查。 大廳外，士兵站在馬匹吃草的草地旁。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Yu had a hunch that the delay originated in a damaged connector, but ordered measurements. An engineer's intellect was useful only when paired with the discipline to stop unsafe work. A patriotic display once used nationalism to justify claiming every coastal object for the crown. A loose ceiling ornament fell after the western roof tiles were torn away. The river guard offered a salute to the visiting Hill Council delegates before the joint inspection. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 陳宇直覺延遲源於損壞接頭，仍下令實測。 工程師的才智只有配合停止危險作業的紀律才有用。 一個愛國展曾用民族主義合理化王室占有所有沿海物件。 西側屋瓦被掀開後，一件鬆脫天花裝飾掉落。 共同檢查前，河岸衛兵向來訪山地議會代表敬禮。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The planner admitted he had signed sloppy estimates without reading the supporting evidence. One solitary guard watched an elderly farmer tiptoe around a broken stair, then offered help without waiting for an order. Emergency supplies included staple foods for residents who could not leave their homes. The staff council could summon the contractor to explain the roof sensor failure. A child tried to tiptoe past the assembly count, but a volunteer gently called her back. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 他承認自己未讀佐證便簽署草率估算。 一名獨自值守的衛兵看見老農夫踮著腳避開破階梯，沒有等命令便主動幫忙。 緊急物資包含給無法離家的居民的主食。 員工委員會能傳喚承包商解釋屋頂感測器故障。 一個孩子想踮腳溜過集合點名，志工溫柔叫她回來。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。
