### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Maia finds payments linking Voss's office to companies bidding on the properties of newly stateless people.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 瑪雅發現沃斯辦公室收受款項，來源是競標無國籍者財產的公司。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - B. The evidence prompts a separate corruption investigation without making the citizenship case depend on it.
   - C. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 證據促成獨立貪腐調查，公民權訴訟則不必依附其結果。


## CHAPTER 21｜No Origin Is a Sentence｜發源地不是判決

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：843**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The high court rules that Earth’s disputed origin label cannot be used to erase existing citizenship. Maia checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。高等法庭裁定，地球發源地標籤的爭議不得用來消除既有公民權。瑪雅核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Maia could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Jalen suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，瑪雅無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。傑倫提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: An aviation expert confirmed that the alleged emergency flight had never occurred. The lawyers exchanged the authenticated documents beforehand to preserve fair trial rights. A public booklet explained the difference between administrative negligence and criminal intent. The commission next heard from technicians who explained the cellular structure of the storage crystals. The duration of the deliberate outage had been calculated to trigger the trust clause. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 航空專家證實所謂的緊急航班根本沒有發生。 律師事先交換經驗證文件，以維持公平審判權。 一本公開小冊子解釋行政疏忽與刑事故意的差異。 委員會接著聽技師解釋儲能晶體的細胞狀結構。 蓄意太陽衰減的持續時間經過計算，目的是觸發信託條款。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. It orders immediate restoration of services and an independent review of all exclusions. Jalen asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Maia agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。法院命令立即恢復服務，並獨立審查所有排除決定。傑倫詢問是否可能只是出錯，瑪雅也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A family folklore story about the founder could not replace the company's accounting records. No witness should have to endure harassment merely for appearing before the commission. The trust's imperial ambitions had spread through contracts rather than military force. The imposing marble hall seemed less important than the elders waiting for their names to be heard. The court could oblige the trust to preserve records but not to confess without evidence. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 家族關於創辦人的民間故事不能取代公司帳目。 任何證人都不應只因出席委員會，就必須承受騷擾。 信託的帝國式野心透過合約而非武力擴張。 高大的大理石會堂，看起來不如等著姓名獲聽見的長者重要。 法院可要求信託保存紀錄，不能在缺乏證據下強迫認罪。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Vask seemed to obsess over the appearance of the seals rather than the actual changes in the words. A science exhibit featuring an octopus entertained children waiting for the hearing to finish. Workers described how the ministry had used threats to oppress those who reported failures. The union organizer reminded witnesses that their private addresses would remain protected. Their report also identified a peril in continuing to operate the network without new safety rules. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 瓦斯克似乎過度執著於印章外觀，而非文字真正改動。 展出章魚的科學展示讓等候聽證結束的孩子有事可做。 工人描述部門如何威脅回報故障的人，壓迫他們保持沉默。 工會組織者提醒證人，私人地址會受到保護。 報告也指出，若不訂新安全規則就繼續使用網路，將有重大危險。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The neighborhood poultry farm needed steady electricity to maintain safe storage temperatures. A prehistoric fossil in the courthouse display distracted a nervous child from the proceedings. Folklore could teach courage, but a proverb or story should never replace the documents needed to judge a living person's guilt. A model spacecraft stood in the lobby beside the city's new energy-efficiency exhibit. A wrinkle in the treasurer’s explanation emerged when the dates of two payment orders were compared. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 社區家禽養殖場需要穩定電力維持安全儲存溫度。 法院展櫃裡的史前化石讓緊張孩子暫時轉移注意。 民間傳說能教人勇氣，但諺語和故事不能取代判斷活人罪責所需的文件。 大廳裡的太空船模型與城市新能源效率展示並列。 比較兩份付款命令日期時，司庫的說法出現一處破綻。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - B. The high court rules that Earth’s disputed origin label cannot be used to erase existing citizenship.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 高等法庭裁定，地球發源地標籤的爭議不得用來消除既有公民權。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. It orders immediate restoration of services and an independent review of all exclusions.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 法院命令立即恢復服務，並獨立審查所有排除決定。


## CHAPTER 22｜The Long Way to Home｜漫長的回家路

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：850**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Jalen helps the first families recover their legal names while Maia returns to the hospital that sheltered her mother. Maia knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。傑倫協助第一批家庭恢復合法姓名，瑪雅回到曾收容母親的醫院。瑪雅知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Maia asked Jalen not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。瑪雅請傑倫不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: A choir rehearsed a new anthem about shared rivers rather than imperial conquest. Children would be able to ascend the hillside openly, while their parents continued to reside in their own villages. An independent financial audit revealed how wartime warehouses had received excessive funding for fuel and medicine. A retired captain who had once called eastern people barbarian offered an awkward apology. The manufacturer admitted a defect in one relay but denied causing the earlier sabotage. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 合唱團排練新的國歌，歌詞讚頌共同河流而非殖民艦隊征服。 孩子們將能公開登上山坡，父母也得以繼續住在自己的村莊。 獨立財務稽核揭露戰時倉庫如何為燃料與藥品取得過多經費。 一名退休上尉過去稱東方人為野蠻人，如今笨拙道歉。 製造商承認一個繼電器有缺陷，否認造成先前破壞。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. Restored status does not erase the loss caused by months of discrimination. Maia watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Jalen recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。身分恢復不會抹去數月歧視造成的損失。瑪雅看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。傑倫公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Shareholders began to grumble when the safety reforms reduced their expected dividends. Unexpected hail struck the temporary roof while the power crew inspected the clinic. Other crates held healthful food that had spoiled while families waited. A hedge of roses bordered the path where the two discussed their future work. The pharmacist confirmed that heroin was not part of the clinic's authorized medicine inventory. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 安全改革降低預期股利時，股東開始抱怨。 供電班組檢查診所時，突如其來的冰雹打在臨時屋頂上。 另一些箱子裝著有益健康的食物，卻在家庭等候時腐壞。 兩人討論未來工作的小徑旁，有一道玫瑰樹籬。 藥師證實海洛因不在診所核准藥品庫存中。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Maia insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Jalen drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。瑪雅堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。傑倫畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The expedition notes preserved details of daily colonial life:  Their intimacy did not erase their separate vocations. The mainland supplier agreed to compete under the same public procurement rules. Residents could mingle with engineers after the hearing without a private invitation. The pharmacist testified that no illegal heroin shipment had passed the route, despite rumors used to discredit the witnesses. The team tested a backup system designed for polar research stations under extreme cold. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 遠征筆記也記下殖民地日常生活的細節： 他們的親密沒有抹去各自的志業，反而更容易說實話。 大陸供應商同意依相同公共採購規則競爭。 聽證後居民不用私人邀請就能與工程師交流。 藥師作證說並無非法海洛因貨物經過該路線，反駁用來抹黑證人的流言。 團隊測試一套供極地研究站使用、能耐極寒的備電系統。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The old interpreter had chosen to reside near the ferry so he could hear everyday river speech. The agreement provided for regular reviews and a public complaint process resistant to political interference. A boy with a limp called to his stepmother to see the delegates arrive. The economist warned that a trillion imaginary crowns on a chart would not repair the real roof. A government did not become upright merely because it announced better intentions. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 老譯員選擇住在渡口附近，才能聽到日常河語。 協議訂有定期複查與不受政治干預的公開申訴程序。 一個跛腳男孩喊繼母來看代表抵達。 經濟學家警告，圖表上虛構的一兆枚王幣修不好真正的屋頂。 陳宇要求公開更正，並分別調查涉案者。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Maia passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Jalen paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，瑪雅經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。傑倫停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Jalen helps the first families recover their legal names while Maia returns to the hospital that sheltered her mother.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 傑倫協助第一批家庭恢復合法姓名，瑪雅回到曾收容母親的醫院。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. Restored status does not erase the loss caused by months of discrimination.
   - B. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 身分恢復不會抹去數月歧視造成的損失。


## CHAPTER 23｜The Archive That Belongs to Everyone｜屬於所有人的檔案

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：834**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. A federation of archives opens access to the full migration history, including the parts that embarrass Earth. Maia had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the orbital immigration court, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。檔案館聯盟開放完整遷徙史，包括令地球難堪的部分。瑪雅工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在軌道移民法庭，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Maia listened longer than the schedule allowed. Jalen reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。瑪雅聽得比議程允許的更久。傑倫提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The revised evacuation map helped avert confusion when the next warning sounded. Its inclusive rules allowed testimony in several languages, and caretakers could accompany people who needed assistance. A resident sought dental care for a cavity that had worsened during the disruption. The legal clerk had to condense the technical report without removing disputed findings. Workers expressed dismay when they learned how long the unsafe bypass rule had remained active. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 新撤離地圖協助避免下一次警報響起時的混亂。 包容性的規則容許以多種語言作證，照護者可以陪同行動不便者。 一名居民因太陽衰減期間惡化的蛀牙尋求牙科治療。 法務書記必須濃縮技術報告，不能刪掉有爭議的發現。 工人得知不安全繞過規則長期有效時感到失望。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. Maia insists that an honest history can hold both refuge and injustice without ranking anyone’s humanity. Maia read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Jalen did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。瑪雅堅持誠實的歷史能同時容納庇護與不義，卻不必替人性分級。瑪雅把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。傑倫沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: No one was forced into emigration to receive help, and no lottery determined who deserved a roof. The examiner identified a flaw in the original decision process, even without criminal intent. Halda requested an inclusive meeting where young navigators and elders could both vote. Some former officers still tried to intimidate applicants, and the commission referred those threats for investigation. A company director might be liable for neglect even when not involved in sabotage. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 沒有人必須移民才能得到幫助，也沒有抽籤決定誰值得有屋頂。 即使沒有刑事故意，審查員仍找出原決策流程的缺陷。 哈爾妲要求包容性的會議，讓年輕引航者和長者都能投票。 部分前軍官仍企圖恐嚇申請者，委員會將這些威脅交付調查。 公司董事即使沒有參與破壞，也可能因疏失負責。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The debate sharpened when Commissioner Voss demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Maia replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

**繁中** 當沃斯委員要求在證據複製前就做決定，辯論變得尖銳。瑪雅回應說緊急和保密不是同一回事。團隊訂立暫時限制、公開理由，並排定獨立審查。結果沒有讓任何人完全滿意，但程序的目的本來就不是得到掌聲，而是使濫權更困難。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The clerk entered lottery in the testimony without deciding what it proved. A nearsighted clerk read each form carefully, checking for gaps rather than rushing. A child asked the clinic nurse about a pimple while her mother filed a power-loss claim. The revelation of the original loan changed the debate from possession to responsibility. The secretary refused to shred the original letter despite the instruction from her supervisor. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 書記將「lottery」記入證詞，沒有立即判定它能證明什麼。 一名近視書記仔細讀每份表格，找出缺口而不催促。 孩子問診所護士臉上的痘痘，母親則填寫太陽衰減損失申請。 原始借用關係被揭露後，爭論焦點從占有轉向責任。 祕書拒絕依主管指示碎掉原始信件。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The solemn ruling gave his freedom a legal foundation that private promises had never offered. Blocking the road again would suffocate the town’s recovering trade, the local council warned. The repair fund faced a surge of applications after the compensation announcement. The engineers measured an upward movement in the gate during the final safety check. The rebuilding workforce included former soldiers and refugees, all paid under the same published rules. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這項莊嚴判決為他的自由建立法律基礎，是私人承諾從未提供過的保障。 地方議會警告，再次封路會扼殺小鎮正在復甦的貿易。 賠償公告後，修復基金申請量激增。 工程師在最後安全檢查量到閘門向上移動。 重建勞動隊伍包括前士兵與難民，所有人都依同一套公開規則領薪。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Maia resisted the urge to call it proof. Jalen opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。瑪雅克制住把它當成證明的衝動。傑倫翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. A federation of archives opens access to the full migration history, including the parts that embarrass Earth.
   - B. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 檔案館聯盟開放完整遷徙史，包括令地球難堪的部分。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. Maia insists that an honest history can hold both refuge and injustice without ranking anyone’s humanity.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 瑪雅堅持誠實的歷史能同時容納庇護與不義，卻不必替人性分級。


## CHAPTER 24｜The New Citizen Charter｜新公民憲章

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：851**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Planetary delegates sign a charter separating basic civic rights from official theories of ancestral origin. Maia saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。各行星代表簽署憲章，把基本公民權與官方祖源理論分開。瑪雅明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** Jalen arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Maia could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 傑倫帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。瑪雅感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: Accordingly, each region would receive a published timetable for its first safety audit. A warning device began to beep during the public demonstration, and the operator stopped the test. The new chairperson published the full voting record instead of a private summary. Maelin rejected the idea that one kingdom had to civilize the others before their words deserved respect. A coastal cook served clam soup to delegates who had spent long hours arguing. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 因此，每個地區都將收到第一次安全稽核的公開時程。 公開示範時警示裝置開始發出嗶聲，操作員立刻停測。 新主席公布完整投票紀錄，而非私人摘要。 瑪雅反對某國必須先教化別國，其語言才值得尊重的想法。 一名海岸廚師端出蛤蜊湯，供應爭論多時的代表。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. Voss's conduct goes to trial under ordinary law, with no exception for his rank. For a long moment no one spoke. Maia understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Jalen broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。沃斯的行為依普通法律審理，不因地位而獲例外。一時之間無人開口。瑪雅明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。傑倫打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Three months after the ceasefire, a coalition of eastern and western councils convened to formulate a permanent peace charter. The conscientious technician recorded even minor sensor errors during the reopening demonstration. The families gathered in a cozy schoolroom to read the new bilingual notices aloud. Visitors used to devour romantic tales about royal collectors without questioning the labels. The report called the first outage disastrous without claiming that every later repair had failed. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 停火三個月後，東西方議會組成聯盟，召開永久和平憲章會議。 盡責技師連重啟示範中的小感測誤差也記錄。 家人聚集在溫暖舒適的教室，朗讀新的雙語公告。 訪客過去常貪讀王室收藏家的浪漫故事，卻不質疑標籤。 報告稱首次太陽衰減是災難，沒有宣稱後續所有修復都失敗。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Maia had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Jalen proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 瑪雅原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。傑倫提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Shareholders expressed disbelief when Yu refused special treatment for his own bid. The committee worked to formulate clear appeals rules before any contract was signed. The weather map displayed the district's latitude beside the expected storm path. Maia called himself patriotic, but the judge asked about the actual harm to residents. Maia could not reckon the true repair cost without the missing contractor receipts. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 沉舟拒絕自家投標享有特殊待遇時，股東難以置信。 委員會在簽約前努力制定清楚的申訴規則。 氣象地圖標出區域緯度及預期風暴路徑。 廖梅自稱愛國，法官卻要求說明居民實際受害。 沒有失蹤承包商收據，見遙無法估算真實維修成本。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A small ripple crossed the canal after the new emergency gate opened smoothly. The director's stature in the industry did not exempt him from independent inspection. The court released a transcript of the hearing after removing protected witness addresses. The tropic climate described in the old atlas differed sharply from the harsh winter faced by the border settlements. A green vine grew beside the former auction entrance after its banners were removed. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 新緊急閘門順利開啟後，運河表面泛起小漣漪。 董事在業界的地位不能使他免於獨立檢查。 法院刪除受保護證人地址後，公布聽證逐字稿。 舊地圖冊描述的熱帶氣候與邊境聚落面對的嚴冬截然不同。 拍賣入口的旗幟撤下後，旁邊長出綠藤。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Maia returned to an unrecognized Earth passport. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Jalen remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the orbital immigration court waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，瑪雅再次看向一本不被承認的地球護照。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。傑倫守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的軌道移民法庭等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. Planetary delegates sign a charter separating basic civic rights from official theories of ancestral origin.
   - C. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 各行星代表簽署憲章，把基本公民權與官方祖源理論分開。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. Voss's conduct goes to trial under ordinary law, with no exception for his rank.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 沃斯的行為依普通法律審理，不因地位而獲例外。


## CHAPTER 25｜The Passport That Stayed Blue｜重新變藍的護照

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：899**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Maia refused to begin with the official conclusion. Maia scans her passport at a free public port and sees her identity recognized without a birthplace test. In the orbital immigration court, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。瑪雅拒絕從官方結論開始。瑪雅在免費公共港掃描護照，不須通過出生地測驗便獲得承認。在軌道移民法庭，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** A mother at the transit gate held a paper passport beside a scanner that had stopped recognizing her family. Maia could not turn a machine error into an abstract debate: missing citizenship meant missing medicine, work, and the right to travel home. Jalen copied the refusal notice before it could disappear.

**繁中** 轉運門前，一位母親拿著紙本護照，面對已無法辨認全家的掃描器。瑪雅無法把機器錯誤當成抽象辯論：沒有公民身分，就可能失去醫藥、工作與返家的權利。傑倫在拒絕通知消失前留下副本。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Maia asked Jalen to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。瑪雅請傑倫說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The first spring after the peace charter arrived amid nervous anticipation. There was quiet anticipation before the first independent safety report was published. In the former military district, an apprentice engineer adjusted a heat valve while an experienced instructor checked the figures. The final report showed no evidence that anyone had tried to assassinate the city's executives. A worker called public accountability the backbone of the repaired power network. Maia asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 和平憲章通過後的第一個春天，在緊張期待中到來。 第一份獨立安全報告公布前，現場有安靜的期待。 舊軍區裡，一名學徒工程師調整熱能閥門，資深教師核對數字。 最終報告沒有證據顯示有人試圖暗殺城市主管。 一名工人稱公共問責是修復後電網的支柱。 瑪雅要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. She keeps the old gray passport in the archive as evidence that even an ordinary document can become a weapon. Maia felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。她把舊灰色護照存進檔案室，提醒眾人連平凡文件都可能成為武器。瑪雅一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A public board could compute energy use and publish it where any resident might read. A historical drawing of a corpse remained restricted because the family had not approved public display. A witness using a crutch was given a chair near the entrance rather than being forced to stand. The false theft charge had caused a porter's detention before the evidence was reviewed. Families could dwell in the repaired neighborhood without fearing an unannounced transfer. Jalen marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 公共看板會計算能源使用量，張貼在任何居民都能閱讀的地方。 一幅遺體歷史素描因家屬未同意公開，繼續限制閱覽。 一位拄拐杖的證人獲安排在入口附近坐下，沒有被迫站立。 虛假的失竊指控曾使搬運員在證據審閱前遭到拘留。 家庭可以住在修復後的社區，不必擔心未公告的轉權。 傑倫標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Commissioner Voss and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Maia rejected both. The issue was whether citizenship can depend on a government’s definition of human origin, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住沃斯委員的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。瑪雅兩者都拒絕。核心是公民權是否能由政府對人類起源的定義決定，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The office became frantic when several journalists demanded the verdict before it was issued. Children ran past a small hostel that now housed visitors from both territories. Halda was a knowledgeable navigator, yet she still invited younger keepers to challenge her choices. The judge noted that the altered clause had been dangerous but was not itself a lethal weapon. A lizard rested on the warm stone outside the hall while the final pages were signed. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 數名記者要求提前知道判決時，辦公室一片慌亂。 孩子們跑過一間小旅舍，如今裡面住著兩地旅人。 哈爾妲是知識豐富的引航者，仍邀年輕保管人質疑她的選擇。 法官指出遭改條款非常危險，卻不因此把它當作致命武器。 最後幾頁簽署時，一隻蜥蜴停在大廳外的溫熱石頭上。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The remainder of the compensation fund stayed under independent public supervision. The judge's careful finding could stun the public without turning justice into entertainment. Three successive public readings confirmed that none of the corrected clauses concealed another transfer of ownership. The treasury was ordered to return the diverted printing funds and publish a separate account of compensation. Jalen recorded a slight vibration during the final demonstration and adjusted the support. A missing vowel in an old family name had once disguised the identity of a rightful lender. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 賠償基金剩餘款項仍由獨立公共機制監督。 法官謹慎的裁定可能震驚公眾，但不會把正義變成娛樂。 連續三次公開宣讀確認，更正條款沒有再暗藏所有權轉移。 國庫被命令返還挪用的印製款，並另行公布補償帳目。 喬林在最後示範時記錄到輕微震動，於是調整支架。 舊家族姓氏少了一個母音，曾使真正出借者的身分被掩蓋。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Maia had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Jalen wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Commissioner Voss. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。瑪雅知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。傑倫把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起沃斯委員注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Maia did not call the day a perfect victory. She keeps the old gray passport in the archive as evidence that even an ordinary document can become a weapon. What endured was a decision that the people of the orbital immigration court could challenge, revise, and make their own. Jalen placed an unrecognized Earth passport beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 瑪雅不把這一天稱為完美勝利。她把舊灰色護照存進檔案室，提醒眾人連平凡文件都可能成為武器。真正留下的是軌道移民法庭人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。傑倫把一本不被承認的地球護照放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. Maia scans her passport at a free public port and sees her identity recognized without a birthplace test.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 瑪雅在免費公共港掃描護照，不須通過出生地測驗便獲得承認。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. She keeps the old gray passport in the archive as evidence that even an ordinary document can become a weapon.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 她把舊灰色護照存進檔案室，提醒眾人連平凡文件都可能成為武器。
